{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011Cefqd1pW6cPzBUnFGiokg","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01AsoVVk7DFvUXQ1GG9QYbpJ","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/llmtest/evals/20260901-162328/nbody_claude-opus-5-cc-medium_hybrid_r1/nbody/nbody.cpp","content":"// Hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA n-body benchmark.\n//\n// Parallelization strategy:\n//   * MPI     : the body array is block-distributed over ranks; every rank keeps a full\n//               copy of all positions and owns the velocities/integration of its block.\n//               Positions are exchanged once per step with a single MPI_Allgatherv.\n//   * OpenMP  : one host thread per GPU owned by the rank (drives independent CUDA\n//               streams/devices concurrently) plus host-side parallel loops for\n//               packing, serialization and validation.\n//   * CUDA    : the O(N^2) force evaluation, the integration and the potential-energy\n//               evaluation run on the GPUs, using shared-memory tiling. Each thread\n//               accumulates the interactions of one body in ascending j order so the\n//               floating point summation order matches the original serial code.\n\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nconstexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;\nconstexpr double DT = 0.01;\n\n// CUDA tuning parameters\nconstexpr int BLOCK = 128;         // threads per block (also the tile size)\nconstexpr int MIN_THREADS = 65536; // target amount of force threads before splitting j\nconstexpr int MIN_SPLIT_LEN = 512; // minimum j-range handled by one j-split\nconstexpr int MAX_SPLIT = 64;      // maximum number of j-splits\n\nstruct Vec3 {\n    double x, y, z;\n    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}\n};\n\nstruct Body {\n    Vec3 pos;\n    Vec3 vel;\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                                     \\\n    do {                                                                                                     \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                                      \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                            \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_), __FILE__, __LINE__);        \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                                     \\\n        }                                                                                                    \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Device kernels\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Accumulates the force on the bodies [off, off+cnt) from the j-range of this split.\n// Partial results are stored as acc[split * cnt + i]; grid.y selects the split.\n__global__ void forceKernel(const double3* __restrict__ pos, const int n, const int off, const int cnt,\n                            const int splitLen, double3* __restrict__ acc) {\n    __shared__ double3 tile[BLOCK];\n\n    const int il = blockIdx.x * BLOCK + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = il < cnt;\n\n    const int jbeg = blockIdx.y * splitLen;\n    const int jend = min(n, jbeg + splitLen);\n\n    double3 p = make_double3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);\n    if (active) { p = pos[off + il]; }\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n\n    for (int t = jbeg; t < jend; t += BLOCK) {\n        const int j = t + threadIdx.x;\n        if (j < jend) { tile[threadIdx.x] = pos[j]; }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const int lim = min(BLOCK, jend - t);\n        if (active) {\n#pragma unroll 4\n            for (int k = 0; k < lim; ++k) {\n                const double dx = tile[k].x - p.x;\n                const double dy = tile[k].y - p.y;\n                const double dz = tile[k].z - p.z;\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (active) { acc[(size_t)blockIdx.y * cnt + il] = make_double3(Fx, Fy, Fz); }\n}\n\n// Sums the per-split partial forces (in ascending split order) and applies them to the\n// local velocities, then integrates the local positions.\n__global__ void updateKernel(const double3* __restrict__ acc, double3* __restrict__ vel,\n                             double3* __restrict__ pos, const int off, const int cnt, const int nSplit) {\n    const int il = blockIdx.x * BLOCK + threadIdx.x;\n    if (il >= cnt) { return; }\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n    for (int s = 0; s < nSplit; ++s) {\n        const double3 a = acc[(size_t)s * cnt + il];\n        Fx += a.x;\n        Fy += a.y;\n        Fz += a.z;\n    }\n\n    double3 v = vel[il];\n    v.x += DT * Fx;\n    v.y += DT * Fy;\n    v.z += DT * Fz;\n    vel[il] = v;\n\n    double3 p = pos[off + il];\n    p.x += v.x * DT;\n    p.y += v.y * DT;\n    p.z += v.z * DT;\n    pos[off + il] = p;\n}\n\n// Potential energy contribution of every local body i: sum over j > i of -1/dist.\n__global__ void energyKernel(const double3* __restrict__ pos, const int n, const int off, const int cnt,\n                             double* __restrict__ part) {\n    __shared__ double3 tile[BLOCK];\n\n    const int il = blockIdx.x * BLOCK + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = il < cnt;\n    const int i = off + il;\n\n    double3 p = make_double3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);\n    if (active) { p = pos[i]; }\n\n    double e = 0.0;\n    // Only tiles that can contain j > i need to be visited.\n    const int tbeg = active ? ((i + 1) / BLOCK) * BLOCK : n;\n    for (int t = tbeg; t < n; t += BLOCK) {\n        __syncthreads();\n        const int j = t + threadIdx.x;\n        if (j < n) { tile[threadIdx.x] = pos[j]; }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const int lim = min(BLOCK, n - t);\n        for (int k = 0; k < lim; ++k) {\n            if (t + k <= i) { continue; }\n            const double dx = tile[k].x - p.x;\n            const double dy = tile[k].y - p.y;\n            const double dz = tile[k].z - p.z;\n            const double dist = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            e -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (active) { part[il] = e; }\n}\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Per-GPU state\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nstruct GpuCtx {\n    int dev = 0;\n    int off = 0;  // first body owned by this GPU (global index)\n    int cnt = 0;  // number of bodies owned by this GPU\n    int nSplit = 1;\n    int splitLen = 0;\n    double3* d_pos = nullptr;   // all N positions\n    double3* d_vel = nullptr;   // cnt velocities\n    double3* d_acc = nullptr;   // nSplit * cnt partial forces\n    double* d_epart = nullptr;  // cnt potential energy partials\n    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;\n};\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Host-side helpers (unchanged semantics, OpenMP parallel)\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<double>& potentialParts) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + body.vel.y * body.vel.y + body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies), accumulated in ascending\n    // body order from the per-body partial sums computed on the GPUs.\n    for (const double p : potentialParts) {\n        energy += p;\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    int bad = 0;  // 1: non-finite, 2: position out of bounds, 3: velocity out of bounds\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(max : bad)\n    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < n; ++idx) {\n        const auto& body = bodies[idx];\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||\n            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {\n            bad = std::max(bad, 1);\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {\n            bad = std::max(bad, 2);\n        }\n        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {\n            bad = std::max(bad, 3);\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (bad == 1) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n    } else if (bad == 2) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n    } else if (bad == 3) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n    }\n    return bad == 0;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int mpiProvided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &mpiProvided);\n\n    int rank = 0, nRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nRanks);\n\n    int numBodies = 1024;\n    int numSteps = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) { printUsage(argv[0]); }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"N-Body Simulation\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of bodies: %d\\n\", numBodies);\n        printf(\"Number of steps: %d\\n\", numSteps);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    if (numBodies <= 0) {\n        if (rank == 0) { printf(\"Simulation time: 0 ms\\n\"); }\n        MPI_Finalize();\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    const int n = numBodies;\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Distribute the bodies: block decomposition over ranks, then over the\n    // GPUs owned by each rank.\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    std::vector<int> rankOff(nRanks + 1);\n    for (int r = 0; r <= nRanks; ++r) {\n        rankOff[r] = (int)((long long)n * r / nRanks);\n    }\n    const int myOff = rankOff[rank];\n    const int myCnt = rankOff[rank + 1] - myOff;\n\n    // Determine how many GPUs this rank may use: devices are shared out among the\n    // ranks that run on the same node.\n    MPI_Comm nodeComm;\n    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&devCount));\n    if (devCount == 0) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA device available\\n\");\n        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n    }\n\n    // Devices [devBeg, devEnd) belong to this rank.\n    const int devBeg = (int)((long long)devCount * nodeRank / nodeSize);\n    const int devEnd = (int)((long long)devCount * (nodeRank + 1) / nodeSize);\n    int nGpu = devEnd - devBeg;\n    if (nGpu < 1) { nGpu = 1; }  // more ranks than devices: share a device\n    const int devFirst = (nGpu == devEnd - devBeg) ? devBeg : (nodeRank % devCount);\n    if (nGpu > myCnt) { nGpu = std::max(myCnt, 1); }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"MPI ranks: %d, GPUs per rank: %d, OpenMP threads: %d\\n\", nRanks, nGpu, omp_get_max_threads());\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Initialization (identical on every rank, so no broadcast is needed)\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    std::vector<Body> bodies(n);\n    randomizeBodies(bodies);\n\n    double3* h_pos = nullptr;  // all N positions, pinned for fast transfers\n    double3* h_vel = nullptr;  // local velocities of this rank\n    double* h_epart = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(devFirst));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc((void**)&h_pos, schedule(static)\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        h_pos[i] = make_double3(bodies[i].pos.x, bodies[i].pos.y, bodies[i].pos.z);\n    }\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (int i = 0; i < myCnt; ++i) {\n        h_vel[i] = make_double3(bodies[myOff + i].vel.x, bodies[myOff + i].vel.y, bodies[myOff + i].vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Per-GPU decomposition of this rank's block\n    std::vector<GpuCtx> ctx(nGpu);\n    for (int g = 0; g < nGpu; ++g) {\n        ctx[g].dev = devBeg + (nGpu == devEnd - devBeg ? g : 0);\n        if (nGpu != devEnd - devBeg) { ctx[g].dev = devFirst; }\n        ctx[g].off = myOff + (int)((long long)myCnt * g / nGpu);\n        ctx[g].cnt = myOff + (int)((long long)myCnt * (g + 1) / nGpu) - ctx[g].off;\n    }\n\n    // MPI exchange descriptors (in units of doubles)\n    std::vector<int> recvCounts(nRanks), recvDispls(nRanks);\n    for (int r = 0; r < nRanks; ++r) {\n        recvCounts[r] = (rankOff[r + 1] - rankOff[r]) * 3;\n        recvDispls[r] = rankOff[r] * 3;\n    }\n\n    const bool needExchange = (nRanks > 1) || (nGpu > 1);\n\n#pragma omp parallel num_threads(nGpu)\n    {\n        const int g = omp_get_thread_num();\n        GpuCtx& c = ctx[g];\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(c.dev));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&c.stream));\n\n        // Choose the number of j-splits so that enough threads are resident even for\n        // small body counts.\n        int nSplit = 1;\n        if (c.cnt > 0) {\n            nSplit = std::max(1, MIN_THREADS / c.cnt);\n            nSplit = std::min(nSplit, MAX_SPLIT);\n            nSplit = std::min(nSplit, std::max(1, n / MIN_SPLIT_LEN));\n        }\n        int splitLen = (n + nSplit - 1) / nSplit;\n        splitLen = ((splitLen + BLOCK - 1) / BLOCK) * BLOCK;\n        nSplit = (n + splitLen - 1) / splitLen;  // drop splits that ended up empty\n        c.nSplit = nSplit;\n        c.splitLen = splitLen;\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc((void**)&c.d_pos, (size_t)n * * c.cnt * sizeof(double3)));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(c.d_vel, h_vel + (c.off - myOff), (size_t)c.cnt * sizeof(double3),\n                                       cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, c.stream));\n        }\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(c.d_pos, h_pos, (size_t)n * sizeof(double3), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, c.stream));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(c.stream));\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Simulation\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n#pragma omp parallel num_threads(nGpu)\n    {\n        const int g = omp_get_thread_num();\n        GpuCtx& for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {\n            if (c.cnt > 0) {\n                forceKernel<<<forceGrid, BLOCK, 0, c.stream>>>(c.d_pos, n, c.off, c.cnt, c.splitLen, c.d_acc);\n                updateKernel<<<updateGrid, BLOCK, 0, c.stream>>>(c.d_acc, c.d_vel, c.d_pos, c.off, c.cnt, c.nSplit);\n            }\n\n            if (needExchange) {\n                if (c.cnt > 0) {\n                    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(h_pos + c.off, c.d_pos + c.off, (size_t)c.cnt * sizeof(double3),\n                                               cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, c.stream));\n                }\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(c.stream));\n\n#pragma omp barrier\n#pragma omp master\n                {\n                    if (nRanks > 1) {\n                        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DATATYPE_NULL, (double*)h_pos, recvCounts.data(),\n                                       recvDispls.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n                    }\n                }\n#pragma omp barrier\n\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(c.d_pos, h_pos, (size_t)n * sizeof(double3), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice,\n                                           c.stream));\n            }\n        }\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(c.stream));\n    }\n\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    if (rank == 0) { printf(\"Simulation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count()); }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Collect the final state (and the potential energy partials if needed)\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    if (validate) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(devFirst));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc((void**)&h_epart, c.off, c.d_pos + c.off, (size_t)c.cnt * sizeof(double3),\n                                           cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, c.stream));\n            }\n            if (validate) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc((void**)&c.d_epart, (size_t)c.cnt * sizeof(double)));\n                energyKernel<<<dim3((c.cnt + BLOCK - 1) / BLOCK), BLOCK, 0, c.stream>>>(c.d_pos, n, c.off, c.cnt,\n                                                                                        c.d_epart);\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(h_epart + (c.off - myOff), c.d_epart, (size_t)c.cnt * sizeof(double),\n                                           cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, c.stream));\n            }\n        }\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(c.stream));\n    }\n\n    // Gather velocities (and energy partials) of all ranks on rank 0.\n    std::vector<double> allVel;\n    std::vector<double> allEpart;\n    if (nRanks > 1) {\n        if (rank == 0) { allVel.resize((size_t)n * Each thread\n//               accumulates the interactions of one body in ascending j order so the\n//               floating point summation order matches the original serial code.\n\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nconstexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;\nconstexpr double DT = 0.01;\n\n// CUDA tuning parameters\nconstexpr int BLOCK = 128;         // threads per block (also the tile size)\nconstexpr int MIN_THREADS = 65536; // target amount of force threads before splitting j\nconstexpr int MIN_SPLIT_LEN = 512; // minimum j-range handled by one j-split\nconstexpr int MAX_SPLIT = 64;      // maximum number of j-splits\n\nstruct Vec3 {\n    double x, y, z;\n    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}\n};\n\nstruct Body {\n    Vec3 pos;\n    Vec3 vel;\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                                     \\\n    do {                                                                                                     \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                                      \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                            \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_), __FILE__, __LINE__);        \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                                     \\\n        }                                                                                                    \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Device kernels\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Accumulates the force on the bodies [off, off+cnt) from the j-range of this split.\n// Partial results are stored as acc[split * cnt + i]; grid.y selects the split.\n__global__ void forceKernel(const double3* __restrict__ pos, const int n, const int off, const int cnt,\n                            const int splitLen, double3* __restrict__ acc) {\n    __shared__ double3 tile[BLOCK];\n\n    const int il = blockIdx.x * BLOCK + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = il < cnt;\n\n    const int jbeg = blockIdx.y * splitLen;\n    const int jend = min(n, jbeg + splitLen);\n\n    double3 p = make_double3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);\n    if (active) { p = pos[off + il]; }\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n\n    for (int t = jbeg; t < jend; t += BLOCK) {\n        const int j = t + threadIdx.x;\n        if (j < jend) { tile[threadIdx.x] = pos[j]; }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const int lim = min(BLOCK, jend - t);\n        if (active) {\n#pragma unroll 4\n            for (int k = 0; k < lim; ++k) {\n                const double dx = tile[k].x - p.x;\n                const double dy = tile[k].y - p.y;\n                const double dz = tile[k].z - p.z;\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (active) { acc[(size_t)blockIdx.y * cnt + il] = make_double3(Fx, Fy, Fz); }\n}\n\n// Sums the per-split partial forces (in ascending split order) and applies them to the\n// local velocities, then integrates the local positions.\n__global__ void updateKernel(const double3* __restrict__ acc, double3* __restrict__ vel,\n                             double3* __restrict__ pos, const int off, const int cnt, const int nSplit) {\n    const int il = blockIdx.x * BLOCK + threadIdx.x;\n    if (il >= cnt) { return; }\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n    for (int s = 0; s < nSplit; ++s) {\n        const double3 a = acc[(size_t)s * cnt + il];\n        Fx += a.x;\n        Fy += a.y;\n        Fz += a.z;\n    }\n\n    double3 v = vel[il];\n    v.x += DT * Fx;\n    v.y += DT * Fy;\n    v.z += DT * Fz;\n    vel[il] = v;\n\n    double3 p = pos[off + il];\n    p.x += v.x * DT;\n    p.y += v.y * DT;\n    p.z += v.z * DT;\n    pos[off + il] = p;\n}\n\n// Potential energy contribution of every local body i: sum over j > i of -1/dist.\n__global__ void energyKernel(const double3* __restrict__ pos, const int n, const int off, const int cnt,\n                             double* __restrict__ part) {\n    __shared__ double3 tile[BLOCK];\n\n    const int il = blockIdx.x * BLOCK + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = il < cnt;\n    const int i = off + il;\n\n    double3 p = make_double3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);\n    if (active) { p = pos[i]; }\n\n    double e = 0.0;\n    // Only tiles that can contain j > i need to be visited.\n    const int tbeg = active ? ((i + 1) / BLOCK) * BLOCK : n;\n    for (int t = tbeg; t < n; t += BLOCK) {\n        __syncthreads();\n        const int j = t + threadIdx.x;\n        if (j < n) { tile[threadIdx.x] = pos[j]; }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const int lim = min(BLOCK, n - t);\n        for (int k = 0; k < lim; ++k) {\n            if (t + k <= i) { continue; }\n            const double dx = tile[k].x - p.x;\n            const double dy = tile[k].y - p.y;\n            const double dz = tile[k].z - p.z;\n            const double dist = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            e -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (active) { part[il] = e; }\n}\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Per-GPU state\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nstruct GpuCtx {\n    int dev = 0;\n    int off = 0;  // first body owned by this GPU (global index)\n    int cnt = 0;  // number of bodies owned by this GPU\n    int nSplit = 1;\n    int splitLen = 0;\n    double3* d_pos = nullptr;   // all N positions\n    double3* d_vel = nullptr;   // cnt velocities\n    double3* d_acc = nullptr;   // nSplit * cnt partial forces\n    double* d_epart = nullptr;  // cnt potential energy partials\n    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;\n};\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Host-side helpers (unchanged semantics, OpenMP parallel)\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<double>& potentialParts) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + body.vel.y * body.vel.y + body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies), accumulated in ascending\n    // body order from the per-body partial sums computed on the GPUs.\n    for (const double p : potentialParts) {\n        energy += p;\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    int bad = 0;  // 1: non-finite, 2: position out of bounds, 3: velocity out of bounds\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(max : bad)\n    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < n; ++idx) {\n        const auto& body = bodies[idx];\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||\n            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {\n            bad = std::max(bad, 1);\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {\n            bad = std::max(bad, 2);\n        }\n        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {\n            bad = std::max(bad, 3);\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (bad == 1) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n    } else if (bad == 2) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n    } else if (bad == 3) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n    }\n    return bad == 0;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int mpiProvided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &mpiProvided);\n\n    int rank = 0, nRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nRanks);\n\n    int numBodies = 1024;\n    int numSteps = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for }\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"N-Body Simulation\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of bodies: %d\\n\", numBodies);\n        printf(\"Number of steps: %d\\n\", numSteps);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    if (numBodies <= 0) {\n        if (rank == 0) { printf(\"Simulation time: 0 ms\\n\"); }\n        MPI_Finalize();\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    const int n = numBodies;\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Distribute the bodies: block decomposition over ranks, }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Simulation\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n Each thread\n//               accumulates the interactions of one body in ascending j order so the\n//               floating point summation order matches the original serial code.\n\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nconstexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;\nconstexpr double DT = 0.01;\n\n// CUDA tuning parameters\nconstexpr int BLOCK = 128;         // threads per block (also the tile size)\nconstexpr int MIN_THREADS = 65536; // target amount of force threads before splitting j\nconstexpr int MIN_SPLIT_LEN = 512; // minimum j-range handled by one j-split\nconstexpr int MAX_SPLIT = 64;      // maximum number of j-splits\n\nstruct Vec3 {\n    double x, y, z;\n    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}\n};\n\nstruct Body {\n    Vec3 pos;\n    Vec3 vel;\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                                     \\\n    do {                                                                                                     \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                                      \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                            \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_), __FILE__, __LINE__);        \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                                     \\\n        }                                                                                                    \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Device kernels\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Accumulates the force on the bodies [off, off+cnt) from the j-range of this split.\n// Partial results are stored as acc[split * cnt + i]; grid.y selects the split.\n__global__ void forceKernel(const double3* __restrict__ pos, const int n, const int off, const int cnt,\n                            const int splitLen, double3* __restrict__ acc) {\n    __shared__ double3 tile[BLOCK];\n\n    const int il = blockIdx.x * BLOCK + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = il < cnt;\n\n    const int jbeg = blockIdx.y * splitLen;\n    const int jend = min(n, jbeg + splitLen);\n\n    double3 p = make_double3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);\n    if (active) { p = pos[off + il]; }\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n\n    for (int t = jbeg; t < jend; t += BLOCK) {\n        const int j = t + threadIdx.x;\n        if (j < jend) { tile[threadIdx.x] = pos[j]; }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const int lim = min(BLOCK, jend - t);\n        if (active) {\n#pragma unroll 4\n            for (int k = 0; k < lim; ++k) {\n                const double dx = tile[k].x - p.x;\n                const double dy = tile[k].y - p.y;\n                const double dz = tile[k].z - p.z;\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (active) { acc[(size_t)blockIdx.y * cnt + il] = make_double3(Fx, Fy, Fz); }\n}\n\n// Sums the per-split partial forces (in ascending split order) and applies them to the\n// local velocities, then integrates the local positions.\n__global__ void updateKernel(const double3* __restrict__ acc, double3* __restrict__ vel,\n                             double3* __restrict__ pos, const int off, const int cnt, const int nSplit) {\n    const int il = blockIdx.x * BLOCK + threadIdx.x;\n    if (il >= cnt) { return; }\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n    for (int s = 0; s < nSplit; ++s) {\n        const double3 a = acc[(size_t)s * cnt + il];\n        Fx += a.x;\n        Fy += a.y;\n        Fz += a.z;\n    }\n\n    double3 v = vel[il];\n    v.x += DT * Fx;\n    v.y += DT * Fy;\n    v.z += DT * Fz;\n    vel[il] = v;\n\n    double3 p = pos[off + il];\n    p.x += v.x * DT;\n    p.y += v.y * DT;\n    p.z += v.z * DT;\n    pos[off + il] = p;\n}\n\n// Potential energy contribution of every local body i: sum over j > i of -1/dist.\n__global__ void energyKernel(const double3* __restrict__ pos, const int n, const int off, const int cnt,\n                             double* __restrict__ part) {\n    __shared__ double3 tile[BLOCK];\n\n    const int il = blockIdx.x * BLOCK + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = il < cnt;\n    const int i = off + il;\n\n    double3 p = make_double3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);\n    if (active) { p = pos[i]; }\n\n    double e = 0.0;\n    // Only tiles that can contain j > i need to be visited.\n    const int tbeg = active ? ((i + 1) / BLOCK) * BLOCK : n;\n    for (int t = tbeg; t < n; t += BLOCK) {\n        __syncthreads();\n        const int j = t + threadIdx.x;\n        if (j < n) { tile[threadIdx.x] = pos[j]; }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const int lim = min(BLOCK, n - t);\n        for (int k = 0; k < lim; ++k) {\n            if (t + k <= i) { continue; }\n            const double dx = tile[k].x - p.x;\n            const double dy = tile[k].y - p.y;\n            const double dz = tile[k].z - p.z;\n            const double dist = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            e -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (active) { part[il] = e; }\n}\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Per-GPU state\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nstruct GpuCtx {\n    int dev = 0;\n    int off = 0;  // first body owned by this GPU (global index)\n    int cnt = 0;  // number of bodies owned by this GPU\n    int nSplit = 1;\n    int splitLen = 0;\n    double3* d_pos = nullptr;   // all N positions\n    double3* d_vel = nullptr;   // cnt velocities\n    double3* d_acc = nullptr;   // nSplit * cnt partial forces\n    double* d_epart = nullptr;  // cnt potential energy partials\n    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;\n};\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Host-side helpers (unchanged semantics, OpenMP parallel)\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<double>& potentialParts) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + body.vel.y * body.vel.y + body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies), accumulated in ascending\n    // body order from the per-body partial sums computed on the GPUs.\n    for (const double p : potentialParts) {\n        energy += p;\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    int bad = 0;  // 1: non-finite, 2: position out of bounds, 3: velocity out of bounds\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(max : bad)\n    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < n; ++idx) {\n        const auto& body = bodies[idx];\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||\n            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {\n            bad = std::max(bad, 1);\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {\n            bad = std::max(bad, 2);\n        }\n        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {\n            bad = std::max(bad, 3);\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (bad == 1) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n    } else if (bad == 2) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n    } else if (bad == 3) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n    }\n    return bad == 0;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n potentialParts) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + body.vel.y * body.vel.y + body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies), accumulated in ascending\n    // body order from the per-body partial sums computed on the GPUs.\n    for (const double p : potentialParts) {\n        energy += p;\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    int bad = 0;  // 1: non-finite, 2: position out of bounds, 3: velocity out of bounds\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(max : bad)\n    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < n; ++idx) {\n        const auto& body = bodies[idx];\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if       bad = std::max(bad, 1);\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {\n            bad = std::max(bad, 2);\n        }\n        if printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n    } else if (bad == 2) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n    } else if MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &nodeRank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(nodeComm, &nodeSize);\n\n    int devCount = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&devCount));\n    if (devCount == 0) {\n        fprintf(stderr, CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc((void**)&c.d_vel, (size_t)c.cnt * sizeof(double3)));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc((void**)&c.d_acc, (size_t)nSplit * c.cnt * sizeof(double3)));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(c.d_vel, h_vel + (c.off - myOff), (size_t)c.cnt * sizeof(double3),\n                                       cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, c.stream));\n        }\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(c.d_pos, h_pos, (size_t)n * sizeof(double3), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, c.stream));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(c.stream));\n    }\n\n    // MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n#pragma omp parallel num_threads(nGpu)\n    {\n        const int g = omp_get_thread_num();\n        GpuCtx&          CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(h_pos + c.off, c.d_pos + c.off, (size_t)c.cnt * sizeof(double3),\n                                               cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, c.stream));\n Each thread\n//               accumulates the interactions of one body in ascending j order so the\n//               floating point summation order matches the original serial code.\n\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nconstexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;\nconstexpr double DT = 0.01;\n\n// CUDA tuning parameters\nconstexpr int BLOCK = 128;         // threads per block (also the tile size)\nconstexpr int MIN_THREADS = 65536; // target amount of force threads before splitting j\nconstexpr int MIN_SPLIT_LEN = 512; // minimum j-range handled by one j-split\nconstexpr int MAX_SPLIT = {}\n};\n\nstruct Body {\n    Vec3 pos;\n    Vec3 vel;\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) -                                        \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                                      \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                            \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_), __FILE__, __LINE__);        \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                                     \\\n        }                                                                                                    \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Device kernels\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Accumulates the force on the bodies [off, off+cnt) from the j-range of this split.\n// Partial results are stored as acc[split * cnt + i]; grid.y selects the split.\n__global__ void forceKernel(const double3* __restrict__ pos, const int n, const int off, const int cnt,\n                            const int splitLen, double3* __restrict__ acc) {\n    __shared__ double3 tile[BLOCK];\n\n    const int il = blockIdx.x * BLOCK + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = il < cnt;\n\n    const int jbeg = blockIdx.y * splitLen;\n    const int jend = min(n, jbeg + splitLen);\n\n    double3 p = make_double3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);\n    if (active) { p = pos[off + il]; }\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n\n    for (int t = jbeg; t < jend; t += BLOCK) {\n        const int j = t + threadIdx.x;\n        if (j < jend) { tile[threadIdx.x] = pos[j]; }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const int lim sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (active) { acc[(size_t)blockIdx.y * cnt + il] = make_double3(Fx, Fy, Fz); }\n}\n\n// Sums the per-split partial forces (in ascending split order) and applies them to the\n// local velocities, then integrates the local positions.\n__global__ void updateKernel(const double3* __restrict__ acc, double3* __restrict__ vel,\n                             double3* __restrict__ pos, const int off, const int cnt, const int nSplit) {\n    const int il = blockIdx.x * BLOCK + threadIdx.x;\n    if (il >= cnt) { return; }\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n    for (int s = 0; s < nSplit; ++s) {\n        const double3 a = acc[(size_t)s * cnt + il];\n        Fx += a.x;\n        Fy += a.y;\n        Fz += a.z;\n    }\n\n    double3 v = vel[il];\n    v.x += DT * Fx;\n    v.y += DT * Fy;\n    v.z += DT * Fz;\n    vel[il] = v;\n\n    double3 p = pos[off + il];\n    p.x += v.x * DT;\n    p.y += v.y * DT;\n    p.z += v.z * DT;\n    pos[off + il] = p;\n}\n\n// Potential energy contribution of every local body i: sum over j > i of -1/dist.\n__global__ void energyKernel(const double3* __restrict__ pos, const int n, const int off, const int cnt,\n                             double* __restrict__ part) {\n    __shared__ double3 tile[BLOCK];\n\n    const int il = blockIdx.x * BLOCK + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = il < cnt;\n    const int i = off + il;\n\n    double3 p = make_double3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);\n    if (active) { p = pos[i]; }\n\n    double e = 0.0;\n    // Only tiles that can contain a j > i of any body in this block need to be visited.\n    // The bound is block-uniform so that __syncthreads() stays convergent.\n    const int tbeg = ((off + blockIdx.x * BLOCK) / BLOCK) * BLOCK;\n t);\n        for (int k = 0; k < lim; ++k) {\n            if (t + k <= i) { continue; }\n            const double dx = tile[k].x - p.x;\n            const double dy = tile[k].y - p.y;\n            const double dz = tile[k].z - p.z;\n            const double dist = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            e -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (active) { part[il] = e; }\n}\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Per-GPU state\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nstruct GpuCtx {\n    int dev = 0;\n    int off = 0;  // first body owned by this GPU (global index)\n    int cnt = 0;  // number of bodies owned by this GPU\n    int nSplit = 1;\n    int splitLen = 0;\n    double3* d_pos = nullptr;   // all N positions\n    double3* d_vel = nullptr;   // cnt velocities\n    double3* d_acc = nullptr;   // nSplit * cnt partial forces\n    double* d_epart = nullptr;  // cnt potential energy partials\n    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;\n};\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Host-side helpers (unchanged semantics, OpenMP parallel)\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<double>& potentialParts) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + body.vel.y * body.vel.y + body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies), accumulated in ascending\n    // body order from the per-body partial sums computed on the GPUs.\n    for (const double p : potentialParts) {\n        energy += p;\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    int bad = 0;  // 1: non-finite, 2: position out of bounds, 3: velocity out of bounds\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(max : bad)\n    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < n; ++idx) {\n        const auto& body = bodies[idx];\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||\n            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {\n            bad = std::max(bad, 1);\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {\n            bad = std::max(bad, 2);\n        }\n        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {\n            bad = std::max(bad, 3);\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (bad == 1) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n    } else if (bad == 2) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n    } else if (bad == 3) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n    }\n    return bad == 0;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int mpiProvided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &mpiProvided);\n\n    int rank = 0, nRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nRanks);\n\n    int numBodies = 1024;\n    int numSteps = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) { printUsage(argv[0]); }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"N-Body Simulation\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of bodies: %d\\n\", numBodies);\n        printf(\"Number of steps: %d\\n\", numSteps);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    if (numBodies <= 0) {\n        if (rank == 0) { printf(\"Simulation time: 0 ms\\n\"); }\n        MPI_Finalize();\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    const int n = numBodies;\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Distribute the bodies: block decomposition over ranks, then over the\n    // GPUs owned by each rank.\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    std::vector<int> rankOff(nRanks + 1);\n    for (int r = 0; r <= nRanks; ++r) {\n        rankOff[r] = (int)((long long)n * r / nRanks);\n    }\n    const int myOff = rankOff[rank];\n    const int myCnt = rankOff[rank + 1] - myOff;\n\n    // Determine how many GPUs this rank may use: devices are shared out among the\n    // ranks that run on the same node.\n    MPI_Comm nodeComm;\n    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, Each thread\n//               accumulates the interactions of one body in ascending j order so the\n//               floating point summation order matches the original serial code.\n\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nconstexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;\nconstexpr double DT = 0.01;\n\n// CUDA tuning parameters\nconstexpr int BLOCK = 128;         // threads per block (also the tile size)\nconstexpr int MIN_THREADS = 65536; // target amount of force threads before splitting j\nconstexpr int MIN_SPLIT_LEN = 512; // minimum j-range handled by one j-split\nconstexpr int MAX_SPLIT = {}\n};\n\nstruct Body {\n    Vec3 pos;\n    Vec3 vel;\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) -                                        \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                                      \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                            \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_), __FILE__, __LINE__);        \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                                     \\\n        } -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Accumulates the force on the bodies [off, off+cnt) from the j-range of this split.\n// Partial results are stored as acc[split * cnt + i]; grid.y selects the split.\n__global__ void forceKernel(const double3* __restrict__ pos, const int n, const int off, const int cnt,\n                            const int splitLen, double3* __restrict__ acc) {\n    __shared__ double3 tile[BLOCK];\n\n    const int il = blockIdx.x * BLOCK + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = il < cnt;\n\n    const int jbeg = blockIdx.y * splitLen;\n    const int jend = min(n, jbeg + splitLen);\n\n    double3 p = make_double3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);\n    if (active) { p = pos[off + il]; }\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (active) { acc[(size_t)blockIdx.y * cnt + il] = make_double3(Fx, Fy, Fz); }\n}\n\n// Sums the per-split partial forces (in ascending split order) and applies them to the\n// local velocities, then integrates the local positions.\n__global__ void updateKernel(const double3* __restrict__ acc, double3* __restrict__ vel,\n                             double3* __restrict__ pos, const int off, const int cnt, const int nSplit) {\n    const int il = blockIdx.x * BLOCK + threadIdx.x;\n    if (il >= cnt) { return; }\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n    for (int s = 0; s < nSplit; ++s) {\n        const double3 a = acc[(size_t)s * cnt + il];\n        Fx += a.x;\n        Fy += a.y;\n        Fz +=    p.z += v.z * DT;\n    pos[off + il] = p;\n}\n\n// Potential energy contribution of every local body i: sum over j > i of -1/dist.\n__global__ void energyKernel(const double3* __restrict__ pos, const int n, const int off, const int cnt,\n                             double* __restrict__ part) {\n    __shared__ double3 tile[BLOCK];\n\n    const int il = blockIdx.x * BLOCK + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = il < cnt;\n    const int i = off + il;\n\n    double3 p = make_double3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);\n    if (active) { p = pos[i]; }\n\n    double e = 0.0;\n    // Only tiles that can contain a j > i of any body in this block need to be visited.\n    // The bound is block-uniform so that __syncthreads() stays convergent.\n    const int tbeg = ((off + blockIdx.x * BLOCK) / BLOCK) * BLOCK;\n t);\n        for (int k = 0; k < lim; ++k) {\n            if (t + k <= i) { continue; }\n            const double dx = tile[k].x - p.x;\n            const double dy = tile[k].y - p.y;\n            const double dz = tile[k].z - p.z;\n            const double dist = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            e -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (active) { part[il] = e; }\n}\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Per-GPU state\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nstruct GpuCtx {\n    int dev = 0;\n    int off = 0;  // first body owned by this GPU (global index)\n    int cnt = 0;  // number of bodies owned by this GPU\n    int nSplit = 1;\n    int splitLen = 0;\n    double3* d_pos = nullptr;   // all N positions\n    double3* d_vel = nullptr;   // cnt velocities\n    double3* -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Host-side helpers (unchanged semantics, OpenMP parallel)\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<double>& potentialParts) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + body.vel.y * body.vel.y + body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies), accumulated in ascending\n    // body order from the per-body partial sums computed on the GPUs.\n    for (const double p : potentialParts) {\n        energy += p;\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    int bad = 0;  // 1: non-finite, 2: position out of bounds, 3: velocity out of bounds\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(max : bad)\n    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < n; ++idx) {\n        const auto& body = bodies[idx];\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y)                                        \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                                      \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                            \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_), __FILE__, __LINE__);        \\\n tile[k].y - p.y;\n                const double dz = tile[k].z - p.z;\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (active) { acc[(size_t)blockIdx.y * cnt + il] = make_double3(Fx, Fy, Fz); }\n}\n\n// Sums the per-split partial forces (in ascending split order) and applies them to the\n// local velocities, then integrates the local positions.\n__global__ void updateKernel(const double3* __restrict__ acc, double3* __restrict__ vel,\n                             double3* __restrict__ pos, const int off, const int cnt, const int nSplit) {\n    const int il = blockIdx.x * BLOCK + threadIdx.x;\n    if (il >= cnt) { return; }\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n    for (int s = 0; s < nSplit; ++s) {\n        const double3 a = acc[(size_t)s * cnt + il];\n        Fx += a.x;\n        Fy += a.y;\n        Fz += a.z;\n    }\n\n    double3 v = vel[il];\n    v.x += DT * Fx;\n    v.y += DT * Fy;\n    v.z += DT * Fz;\n    vel[il] = v;\n\n    double3 p = pos[off + il];\n    p.x += v.x * DT;\n    p.y += v.y * DT;\n    p.z += v.z * DT;\n    pos[off + il] = p;\n}\n\n// Potential energy contribution of every local body i: sum over j > i of -1/dist.\n__global__ void energyKernel(const double3* __restrict__ pos, const int n, const int off, const int cnt,\n                             double* __restrict__ part) {\n    __shared__ double3 tile[BLOCK];\n\n    const int il = blockIdx.x * BLOCK + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = il < cnt;\n    const int i = off + il;\n\n    double3 p = make_double3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);\n    if (active) { p = pos[i]; }\n\n    double e = 0.0;\n    // Only tiles that can contain a j > i of any body in this block need to be visited.\n    // The bound is block-uniform so that __syncthreads() stays convergent.\n    const int tbeg = ((off + blockIdx.x * BLOCK) / BLOCK) * BLOCK;\n    for (int t = tbeg; t < n; t += BLOCK) {\n        const int j = t + threadIdx.x;\n        if (j < n) { tile[threadIdx.x] = pos[j]; }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const int lim = min(BLOCK, n - t);\n        for (int k = 0; k < lim; ++k) {\n            if (t + k <= i) { continue; }\n            const double dx = tile[k].x - p.x;\n            const double dy = tile[k].y - p.y;\n            const double dz = tile[k].z - p.z;\n            const double dist = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            e -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (active) { part[il] = e; }\n}\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Per-GPU state\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nstruct GpuCtx {\n    int dev = 0;\n    int off = 0;  // first body owned by this GPU (global index)\n    int cnt = 0;  // number of bodies owned by this GPU\n    int nSplit = 1;\n    int splitLen = 0;\n    double3* d_pos = nullptr;   // all N positions\n    double3* d_vel = nullptr;   // cnt velocities\n    double3* d_acc = nullptr;   // nSplit * cnt partial forces\n    double* d_epart = nullptr;  // cnt potential energy partials\n    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;\n};\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Host-side helpers (unchanged semantics, OpenMP parallel)\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<double>& potentialParts) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + body.vel.y * body.vel.y + body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies), accumulated in ascending\n    // body order from the per-body partial sums computed on the GPUs.\n    for (const double p : potentialParts) {\n        energy += p;\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    int bad = 0;  // 1: non-finite, 2: position out of bounds, 3: velocity out of bounds\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(max : bad)\n    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < n; ++idx) {\n        const auto& body = bodies[idx];\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||\n            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {\n            bad = std::max(bad, 1);\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {\n            bad = std::max(bad, 2);\n        }\n        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {\n            bad = std::max(bad, 3);\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (bad == 1) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n    } else if (bad == 2) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n    } else if (bad == 3) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n    }\n    return bad == 0;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int mpiProvided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &mpiProvided);\n\n    int rank = 0, nRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nRanks);\n\n    int numBodies = 1024;\n    int numSteps = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) { printUsage(argv[0]); }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"N-Body Simulation\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of bodies: %d\\n\", numBodies);\n        printf(\"Number of steps: %d\\n\", numSteps);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    if (numBodies <= 0) {\n        if (rank == 0) { printf(\"Simulation time: 0 ms\\n\"); }\n        MPI_Finalize();\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    const int n = numBodies;\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Distribute the bodies: block decomposition over ranks, then over the\n    // GPUs owned by each rank.\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    std::vector<int> rankOff(nRanks + 1);\n    for (int r = 0; r <= nRanks; ++r) {\n        rankOff[r] = (int)((long long)n * r / nRanks);\n    }\n    const int myOff = rankOff[rank];\n    const int myCnt = rankOff[rank + 1] - myOff;\n\n    // Determine how many GPUs this rank may use: devices are shared out among the\n    // ranks that run on the same node.\n    MPI_Comm nodeComm;\n    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm);\n    int nodeRank = 0, nodeSize = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &nodeRank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(nodeComm, &nodeSize);\n\n    int devCount = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&devCount));\n    if (devCount == 0) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA device available\\n\");\n        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n    }\n\n    // Devices [devBeg, devEnd) belong to this rank; if there are more ranks than\n    // devices on the node, the ranks share devices round-robin.\n    std::vector<int> myDevs;\n    const int devBeg = (int)((long long)devCount * nodeRank / nodeSize);\n    const int devEnd = (int)((long long)devCount * (nodeRank + 1) / nodeSize);\n    if (devEnd > devBeg) {\n        for (int d = devBeg; d < devEnd; ++d) {\n            myDevs.push_back(d);\n        }\n    } else {\n        myDevs.push_back(nodeRank % devCount);\n    }\n    // Never use more GPUs than there are bodies to compute.\n    if ((int)myDevs.size() > myCnt && myCnt > 0) { myDevs.resize(myCnt); }\n    const int nGpu = (int)myDevs.size();\n    const int devFirst = myDevs[0];\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"MPI ranks: %d, GPUs per rank: %d, OpenMP threads: %d\\n\", nRanks, nGpu, omp_get_max_threads());\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Initialization (identical on every rank, so no broadcast is needed)\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    std::vector<Body> bodies(n);\n    randomizeBodies(bodies);\n\n    double3* h_pos = nullptr;  // all N positions, pinned for fast transfers\n    double3* h_vel = nullptr;  // local velocities of this rank\n    double* h_epart = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(devFirst));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc((void**)&h_pos, (size_t)n * sizeof(double3), cudaHostAllocPortable));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc((void**)&h_vel, (size_t)std::max(myCnt, 1) * sizeof(double3), cudaHostAllocPortable));\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        h_pos[i] = make_double3(bodies[i].pos.x, bodies[i].pos.y, bodies[i].pos.z);\n    }\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (int i = 0; i < myCnt; ++i) {\n        h_vel[i] = make_double3(bodies[myOff + i].vel.x, bodies[myOff + i].vel.y, bodies[myOff + i].vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Per-GPU decomposition of this rank's block\n    std::vector<GpuCtx> ctx(nGpu);\n    for (int g = 0; g < nGpu; ++g) {\n        ctx[g].dev = myDevs[g];\n        ctx[g].off = myOff + (int)((long long)myCnt * g / nGpu);\n        ctx[g].cnt = myOff + (int)((long long)myCnt * (g + 1) / nGpu) - ctx[g].off;\n    }\n\n    // MPI exchange descriptors (in units of doubles)\n    std::vector<int> recvCounts(nRanks), recvDispls(nRanks);\n    for (int r = 0; r < nRanks; ++r) {\n        recvCounts[r] = (rankOff[r + 1] - rankOff[r]) * 3;\n        recvDispls[r] = rankOff[r] * 3;\n    }\n\n    const bool needExchange = (nRanks > 1) || (nGpu > 1);\n\n#pragma omp parallel num_threads(nGpu)\n    {\n        const int g = omp_get_thread_num();\n        GpuCtx& c = ctx[g];\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(c.dev));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&c.stream));\n\n        // Choose the number of j-splits so that enough threads are resident even for\n        // small body counts.\n        int nSplit = 1;\n        if (c.cnt > 0) {\n            nSplit = std::max(1, MIN_THREADS / c.cnt);\n            nSplit = std::min(nSplit, MAX_SPLIT);\n            nSplit = std::min(nSplit, std::max(1, n / MIN_SPLIT_LEN));\n        }\n        int splitLen = (n + nSplit - 1) / nSplit;\n        splitLen = ((splitLen + BLOCK - 1) / BLOCK) * BLOCK;\n        nSplit = (n + splitLen - 1) / splitLen;  // drop splits that ended up empty\n        c.nSplit = nSplit;\n        c.splitLen = splitLen;\n\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc((void**)&c.d_pos, (size_t)n * sizeof(double3)));\n        if (c.cnt > 0) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc((void**)&c.d_vel, (size_t)c.cnt * sizeof(double3)));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc((void**)&c.d_acc, (size_t)nSplit * c.cnt * sizeof(double3)));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(c.d_vel, h_vel + (c.off - myOff), (size_t)c.cnt * sizeof(double3),\n                                       cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, c.stream));\n        }\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(c.d_pos, h_pos, (size_t)n * sizeof(double3), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, c.stream));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(c.stream));\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Simulation\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n#pragma omp parallel num_threads(nGpu)\n    {\n        const int g = omp_get_thread_num();\n        GpuCtx& c = ctx[g];\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(c.dev));\n\n        const dim3 forceGrid((c.cnt + BLOCK - 1) / BLOCK, c.nSplit);\n        const dim3 updateGrid((c.cnt + BLOCK - 1) / BLOCK);\n\n        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {\n            if (c.cnt > 0) {\n                forceKernel<<<forceGrid, BLOCK, 0, c.stream>>>(c.d_pos, n, c.off, c.cnt, c.splitLen, c.d_acc);\n                updateKernel<<<updateGrid, BLOCK, 0, c.stream>>>(c.d_acc, c.d_vel, c.d_pos, c.off, c.cnt, c.nSplit);\n            }\n\n            if (needExchange) {\n                if (c.cnt > 0) {\n                    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(h_pos + c.off, c.d_pos + c.off, (size_t)c.cnt * sizeof(double3),\n                                               cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, c.stream));\n                }\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(c.stream));\n\n#pragma omp barrier\n#pragma omp master\n                {\n                    if (nRanks > 1) {\n                        MPI_Allgatherv(MPI_IN_PLACE, 0, MPI_DATATYPE_NULL, (double*)h_pos, recvCounts.data(),\n                                       recvDispls.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n                    }\n                }\n#pragma omp barrier\n\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(c.d_pos, h_pos, (size_t)n * sizeof(double3), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice,\n                                           c.stream));\n            }\n        }\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(c.stream));\n    }\n\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    if (rank == 0) { printf(\"Simulation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count()); }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Collect the final state (and the potential energy partials if needed)\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    if (validate) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(devFirst));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc((void**)&h_epart, (size_t)std::max(myCnt, 1) * sizeof(double),\n                                 cudaHostAllocPortable));\n    }\n\n#pragma omp parallel num_threads(nGpu)\n    {\n        const int g = omp_get_thread_num();\n        GpuCtx& c = ctx[g];\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(c.dev));\n        if (c.cnt > 0) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(h_vel + (c.off - myOff), c.d_vel, (size_t)c.cnt * sizeof(double3),\n                                       cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, c.stream));\n            if (!needExchange) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(h_pos + c.off, c.d_pos + c.off, (size_t)c.cnt * sizeof(double3),\n                                           cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, c.stream));\n            }\n            if (validate) {\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc((void**)&c.d_epart, (size_t)c.cnt * sizeof(double)));\n                energyKernel<<<dim3((c.cnt + BLOCK - 1) / BLOCK), BLOCK, 0, c.stream>>>(c.d_pos, n, c.off, c.cnt,\n                                                                                        c.d_epart);\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(h_epart + (c.off - myOff), c.d_epart, (size_t)c.cnt * sizeof(double),\n                                           cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, c.stream));\n            }\n        }\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(c.stream));\n    }\n\n    // Gather velocities (and energy partials) of all ranks on rank 0.\n    std::vector<double> allVel;\n    std::vector<double> allEpart;\n    if (nRanks > 1) {\n        if (rank == 0) { allVel.resize((size_t)n *
