Each thread\n//               accumulates the interactions of one body in ascending j order so the\n//               floating point summation order matches the original serial code.\n\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nconstexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;\nconstexpr double DT = 0.01;\n\n// CUDA tuning parameters\nconstexpr int BLOCK = 128;         // threads per block (also the tile size)\nconstexpr int MIN_THREADS = 65536; // target amount of force threads before splitting j\nconstexpr int MIN_SPLIT_LEN = 512; // minimum j-range handled by one j-split\nconstexpr int MAX_SPLIT = 64;      // maximum number of j-splits\n\nstruct Vec3 {\n    double x, y, z;\n    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}\n};\n\nstruct Body {\n    Vec3 pos;\n    Vec3 vel;\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                                     \\\n    do {                                                                                                     \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                                      \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                            \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_), __FILE__, __LINE__);        \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                                     \\\n        }                                                                                                    \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Device kernels\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Accumulates the force on the bodies [off, off+cnt) from the j-range of this split.\n// Partial results are stored as acc[split * cnt + i]; grid.y selects the split.\n__global__ void forceKernel(const double3* __restrict__ pos, const int n, const int off, const int cnt,\n                            const int splitLen, double3* __restrict__ acc) {\n    __shared__ double3 tile[BLOCK];\n\n    const int il = blockIdx.x * BLOCK + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = il < cnt;\n\n    const int jbeg = blockIdx.y * splitLen;\n    const int jend = min(n, jbeg + splitLen);\n\n    double3 p = make_double3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);\n    if (active) { p = pos[off + il]; }\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n\n    for (int t = jbeg; t < jend; t += BLOCK) {\n        const int j = t + threadIdx.x;\n        if (j < jend) { tile[threadIdx.x] = pos[j]; }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const int lim = min(BLOCK, jend - t);\n        if (active) {\n#pragma unroll 4\n            for (int k = 0; k < lim; ++k) {\n                const double dx = tile[k].x - p.x;\n                const double dy = tile[k].y - p.y;\n                const double dz = tile[k].z - p.z;\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (active) { acc[(size_t)blockIdx.y * cnt + il] = make_double3(Fx, Fy, Fz); }\n}\n\n// Sums the per-split partial forces (in ascending split order) and applies them to the\n// local velocities, then integrates the local positions.\n__global__ void updateKernel(const double3* __restrict__ acc, double3* __restrict__ vel,\n                             double3* __restrict__ pos, const int off, const int cnt, const int nSplit) {\n    const int il = blockIdx.x * BLOCK + threadIdx.x;\n    if (il >= cnt) { return; }\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n    for (int s = 0; s < nSplit; ++s) {\n        const double3 a = acc[(size_t)s * cnt + il];\n        Fx += a.x;\n        Fy += a.y;\n        Fz += a.z;\n    }\n\n    double3 v = vel[il];\n    v.x += DT * Fx;\n    v.y += DT * Fy;\n    v.z += DT * Fz;\n    vel[il] = v;\n\n    double3 p = pos[off + il];\n    p.x += v.x * DT;\n    p.y += v.y * DT;\n    p.z += v.z * DT;\n    pos[off + il] = p;\n}\n\n// Potential energy contribution of every local body i: sum over j > i of -1/dist.\n__global__ void energyKernel(const double3* __restrict__ pos, const int n, const int off, const int cnt,\n                             double* __restrict__ part) {\n    __shared__ double3 tile[BLOCK];\n\n    const int il = blockIdx.x * BLOCK + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = il < cnt;\n    const int i = off + il;\n\n    double3 p = make_double3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);\n    if (active) { p = pos[i]; }\n\n    double e = 0.0;\n    // Only tiles that can contain a j > i of any body in this block need to be visited.\n    // The bound is block-uniform so that __syncthreads() stays convergent.\n    const int tbeg = ((off + blockIdx.x * BLOCK) / BLOCK) * BLOCK;\n    for (int t = tbeg; t < n; t += BLOCK) {\n        const int j = t + threadIdx.x;\n        if (j < n) { tile[threadIdx.x] = pos[j]; }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const int lim = min(BLOCK, n - const double dz = tile[k].z - p.z;\n            const double dist = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            e -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (active) { part[il] = e; }\n}\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Per-GPU state\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nstruct GpuCtx {\n    int dev = 0;\n    int off = 0;  // first body owned by this GPU (global index)\n    int cnt = 0;  // number of bodies owned by this GPU\n    int nSplit = 1;\n    int splitLen = 0;\n    double3* d_pos = nullptr;   // all N positions\n    double3* d_vel = nullptr;   // cnt velocities\n    double3* d_acc = nullptr;   // nSplit * cnt partial forces\n    double* d_epart = nullptr;  // cnt potential energy partials\n    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;\n};\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Host-side helpers (unchanged semantics, OpenMP parallel)\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<double>& potentialParts) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + body.vel.y * body.vel.y + body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies), accumulated in ascending\n    // body order from the per-body partial sums computed on the GPUs.\n    for (const double p : potentialParts) {\n        energy += p;\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    int bad = 0;  // 1: non-finite, 2: position out of bounds, 3: velocity out of bounds\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(max : bad)\n    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < n; ++idx) {\n        const auto& body = bodies[idx];\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||\n            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {\n            bad = std::max(bad, 1);\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {\n            bad = std::max(bad, 2);\n        }\n        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {\n            bad = std::max(bad, 3);\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (bad == 1) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n    } else if (bad == 2) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n    } else if (bad == 3) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n    }\n    return bad == 0;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int mpiProvided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &mpiProvided);\n    (void)mpiProvided;  // MPI calls are funnelled through the master thread\n\n    int rank = 0, nRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nRanks);\n\n    int numBodies = 1024;\n    int numSteps = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) { printUsage(argv[0]); }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"N-Body Simulation\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of bodies: %d\\n\", numBodies);\n        printf(\"Number of steps: %d\\n\", numSteps);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    if (numBodies <= 0) {\n        if (rank == 0) { printf(\"Simulation time: 0 ms\\n\"); }\n        MPI_Finalize();\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    const int n = numBodies;\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Distribute the bodies: block decomposition over ranks, then over the\n    // GPUs owned by each rank.\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    std::vector<int> rankOff(nRanks + 1);\n    for (int r = 0; r <= nRanks; ++r) {\n        rankOff[r] = (int)((long long)n * r / nRanks);\n    }\n    const int myOff = rankOff[rank];\n    const int myCnt = rankOff[rank + 1] - myOff;\n\n    // Determine how many GPUs this rank may use: devices are shared out among the\n    // ranks that run on the same node.\n    MPI_Comm nodeComm;\n    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm);\n    int nodeRank = 0, nodeSize = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &nodeRank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(nodeComm, &nodeSize);\n\n    int devCount = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&devCount));\n    if (devCount == 0) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA device available\\n\");\n        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n    }\n\n    // Devices [devBeg, devEnd) belong to this rank; if there are more ranks than\n    // devices on the node, the ranks share devices round-robin.\n    std::vector<int> myDevs;\n    const int devBeg = (int)((long long)devCount * nodeRank / nodeSize);\n    const int devEnd = (int)((long long)devCount * (nodeRank + 1) / nodeSize);\n    if (devEnd > devBeg) {\n        for (int d = devBeg; d < devEnd; ++d) {\n            myDevs.push_back(d);\n        }\n    } else {\n        myDevs.push_back(nodeRank % devCount);\n    }\n    // Never use more GPUs than there are bodies to compute.\n    if ((int)myDevs.size() > myCnt && myCnt > 0) { myDevs.resize(myCnt); }\n    const int nGpu = (int)myDevs.size();\n    const int devFirst = myDevs[0];\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"MPI ranks: %d, GPUs per rank: %d, OpenMP threads: %d\\n\", nRanks, nGpu, omp_get_max_threads());\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Initialization (identical on every rank, so no broadcast is needed)\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    std::vector<Body> bodies(n);\n    randomizeBodies(bodies);\n\n    double3* h_pos = nullptr;  // all N positions, pinned for fast transfers\n    double3* h_vel = nullptr;  // local velocities of this rank\n    double* h_epart = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(devFirst));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc((void**)&h_pos, (size_t)n * sizeof(double3), cudaHostAllocPortable));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc((void**)&h_vel, (size_t)std::max(myCnt, 1) * sizeof(double3), cudaHostAllocPortable));\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        h_pos[i] = make_double3(bodies[i].pos.x, bodies[i].pos.y, bodies[i].pos.z);\n    }\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (int blockIdx.y * splitLen;\n    const int jend = min(n, jbeg + splitLen);\n\n    double3 p = make_double3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);\n    if (active) { p = pos[off + il]; }\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n\n    for (int t = jbeg; t < jend; t += BLOCK) {\n        const int j = t + threadIdx.x;\n        if (j < jend) { tile[threadIdx.x] = pos[j]; }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const int lim = min(BLOCK, jend - t);\n        if (active) {\n#pragma unroll 4\n            for (int k = 0; k < lim; ++k) {\n                const double dx = tile[k].x - p.x;\n                const double dy = tile[k].y - p.y;\n                const double dz = tile[k].z - p.z;\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                // rsqrt() is the double precision reciprocal square root (<= 1 ulp,\n                // i.e. the same accuracy as 1.0/sqrt(x) but far cheaper).\n                const double invDist = rsqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (active) { acc[(size_t)blockIdx.y * cnt + il] = make_double3(Fx, Fy, Fz); }\n}\n\n// Sums the per-split partial forces (in ascending split order) and applies them to the\n// local velocities, then integrates the local positions.\n__global__ void updateKernel(const double3* __restrict__ acc, double3* __restrict__ vel,\n                             double3* __restrict__ pos, const int off, const int cnt, const int nSplit) {\n    const int il = blockIdx.x * BLOCK + threadIdx.x;\n    if (il >= cnt) { return; }\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n    for (int s = 0; s < nSplit; ++s) {\n        const double3 a = acc[(size_t)s * cnt + il];\n        Fx += a.x;\n        Fy += a.y;\n        Fz += a.z;\n    }\n\n    double3 v = vel[il];\n    v.x += DT * Fx;\n    v.y += DT * Fy;\n    v.z += DT * Fz;\n    vel[il] = v;\n\n    double3 p = pos[off + il];\n    p.x += v.x * DT;\n    p.y += v.y * DT;\n    p.z += v.z * DT;\n    pos[off + il] = p;\n}\n\n// Potential energy contribution of every local body i: sum over j > i of -1/dist.\n__global__ void energyKernel(const double3* __restrict__ pos, const int n, const int off, const int cnt,\n                             double* __restrict__ part) {\n    __shared__ double3 tile[BLOCK];\n\n    const int il = blockIdx.x * BLOCK + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = il < cnt;\n    const int i = off + il;\n\n    double3 p = make_double3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);\n    if (active) { p = pos[i]; }\n\n    double e = 0.0;\n    // Only tiles that can contain a j > i of any body in this block need to be visited.\n    // The bound is block-uniform so that __syncthreads() stays convergent.\n    const int tbeg = ((off + blockIdx.x * BLOCK) / BLOCK) * BLOCK;\n    for (int t = tbeg; t < n; t += BLOCK) {\n        const int j = t + threadIdx.x;\n        if (j < n) { tile[threadIdx.x] = pos[j]; }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const int lim = min(BLOCK, n - t);\n        for (int k = 0; k < lim; ++k) {\n            if (t + k <= i) { continue; }\n            const double dx = tile[k].x - p.x;\n            const double dy = tile[k].y - p.y;\n            const double dz = tile[k].z - p.z;\n            const double dist = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            e -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (active) { part[il] = e; }\n}\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Per-GPU state\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nstruct GpuCtx {\n    int dev = 0;\n    int off = 0;  // first body owned by this GPU (global index)\n    int cnt = 0;  // number of bodies owned by this GPU\n    int nSplit = 1;\n    int splitLen = 0;\n    double3* d_pos = nullptr;   // all N positions\n    double3* d_vel = nullptr;   // cnt velocities\n    double3* d_acc = nullptr;   // nSplit * cnt partial forces\n    double* d_epart = nullptr;  // cnt potential energy partials\n    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;\n};\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Host-side helpers (unchanged semantics, OpenMP parallel)\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<double>& potentialParts) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + body.vel.y * body.vel.y + body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies), accumulated in ascending\n    // body order from the per-body partial sums computed on the GPUs.\n    for (const double p : potentialParts) {\n        energy += p;\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    int bad = 0;  // 1: non-finite, 2: position out of bounds, 3: velocity out of bounds\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(max : bad)\n    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < n; ++idx) {\n        const auto& body = bodies[idx];\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||\n            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {\n            bad = std::max(bad, 1);\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {\n            bad = std::max(bad, 2);\n        }\n        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {\n            bad = std::max(bad, 3);\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (bad == 1) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n    } else if (bad == 2) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n    } else if (bad == 3) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n    }\n    return bad == 0;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n Parallelization strategy:\n//   * MPI     : the body array is block-distributed over ranks; every rank keeps a full\n//               copy of all positions and owns the velocities/integration of its block.\n//               Positions are exchanged once per step with a single MPI_Allgatherv.\n//   * OpenMP  : one host thread per GPU owned by the rank (drives independent CUDA\n//               streams/devices concurrently) plus host-side parallel loops for\n//               packing, serialization and validation.\n//   * CUDA    : the O(N^2) force evaluation, the integration and the potential-energy\n//               evaluation run on the GPUs, using shared-memory tiling. Each thread\n//               accumulates the interactions of one body in ascending j order so the\n//               floating point summation order matches the original serial code.\n\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nconstexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;\nconstexpr double DT = 0.01;\n\n// CUDA tuning parameters\nconstexpr int BLOCK = 128;         // threads per block (also the tile size)\nconstexpr int MIN_THREADS = 65536; // target amount of force threads before splitting j\nconstexpr int MIN_SPLIT_LEN = 512; // minimum j-range handled by one j-split\nconstexpr int MAX_SPLIT = 64;      // maximum number of j-splits\n\nstruct Vec3 {\n    double x, y, z;\n    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}\n};\n\nstruct Body {\n    Vec3 pos;\n    Vec3 vel;\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                                     \\\n    do {                                                                                                     \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                                      \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                            \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_), __FILE__, __LINE__);        \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                                     \\\n        }                                                                                                    \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Device kernels\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Accumulates the force on the bodies [off, off+cnt) from the j-range of this split.\n// Partial results are stored as acc[split * cnt + i]; grid.y selects the split.\n__global__ void forceKernel(const double3* __restrict__ pos, const int n, const int off, const int cnt,\n                            const int splitLen, double3* __restrict__ acc) {\n    __shared__ double3 tile[BLOCK];\n\n    const int il = blockIdx.x * BLOCK + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = il < cnt;\n\n    const int jbeg = blockIdx.y * splitLen;\n    const int jend = min(n, jbeg + splitLen);\n\n    double3 p = make_double3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);\n    if (active) { p = pos[off + il]; }\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n\n    for (int t = jbeg; t < jend; t += BLOCK) {\n        const int j = t + threadIdx.x;\n        if (j < jend) { tile[threadIdx.x] = pos[j]; }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const int lim = min(BLOCK, jend - t);\n        if (active) {\n#pragma unroll 4\n            for (int k = 0; k < lim; ++k) {\n                const double dx = tile[k].x - p.x;\n                const double dy = tile[k].y - p.y;\n                const double dz = tile[k].z - p.z;\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                // rsqrt() is the double precision reciprocal square root (<= 1 ulp,\n                // i.e. the same accuracy as 1.0/sqrt(x) but far cheaper).\n                const double invDist = rsqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (active) { acc[(size_t)blockIdx.y * cnt + il] = make_double3(Fx, Fy, Fz); }\n}\n\n// Sums the per-split partial forces (in ascending split order) and applies them to the\n// local velocities, then integrates the local positions.\n__global__ void updateKernel(const double3* __restrict__ acc, double3* __restrict__ vel,\n                             double3* __restrict__ pos, const int off, const int cnt, const int nSplit) {\n    const int il = blockIdx.x * BLOCK + threadIdx.x;\n    if (il >= cnt) { return; }\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n    for (int s = 0; s < nSplit; ++s) {\n        const double3 a = acc[(size_t)s * cnt + il];\n        Fx += a.x;\n        Fy += a.y;\n        Fz += a.z;\n    }\n\n    double3 v = vel[il];\n    v.x += DT * Fx;\n    v.y += DT * Fy;\n    v.z += DT * Fz;\n    vel[il] = v;\n\n    double3 p = pos[off + il];\n    p.x += v.x * DT;\n    p.y += v.y * DT;\n    p.z += v.z * DT;\n    pos[off + il] = p;\n}\n\n// Potential energy contribution of every local body i: sum over j > i of -1/dist.\n__global__ void energyKernel(const double3* __restrict__ pos, const int n, const int off, const int cnt,\n                             double* __restrict__ part) {\n    __shared__ double3 tile[BLOCK];\n\n    const int il = blockIdx.x * BLOCK + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = il < cnt;\n    const int i = off + il;\n\n    double3 p = make_double3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);\n    if (active) { p = pos[i]; }\n\n    double e = 0.0;\n    // Only tiles that can contain a j > i of any body in this block need to be visited.\n    // The bound is block-uniform so that __syncthreads() stays convergent.\n    const int tbeg = ((off + blockIdx.x * BLOCK) / BLOCK) * BLOCK;\n    for (int t = tbeg; t < n; t += BLOCK) {\n        const int j = t + threadIdx.x;\n        if (j < n) { tile[threadIdx.x] = pos[j]; }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const int lim = min(BLOCK, n - t);\n        for (int k = 0; k < lim; ++k) {\n            if (t + k <= i) { continue; }\n            const double dx = tile[k].x - p.x;\n            const double dy = tile[k].y - p.y;\n            const double dz = tile[k].z - p.z;\n            const double dist = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            e -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (active) { part[il] = e; }\n}\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Per-GPU state\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nstruct GpuCtx {\n    int dev = 0;\n    int off = 0;  // first body owned by this GPU (global index)\n    int cnt = 0;  // number of bodies owned by this GPU\n    int nSplit = 1;\n    int splitLen = 0;\n    double3* d_pos = nullptr;   // all N positions\n    double3* d_vel = nullptr;   // cnt velocities\n    double3* d_acc = nullptr;   // nSplit * cnt partial forces\n    double* d_epart = nullptr;  // cnt potential energy partials\n    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;\n};\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Host-side helpers (unchanged semantics, OpenMP parallel)\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<double>& potentialParts) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + body.vel.y * body.vel.y + body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies), accumulated in ascending\n    // body order from the per-body partial sums computed on the GPUs.\n    for (const double p : potentialParts) {\n        energy += p;\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    int bad = 0;  // 1: non-finite, 2: position out of bounds, 3: velocity out of bounds\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(max : bad)\n    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < n; ++idx) {\n        const auto& body = bodies[idx];\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||\n            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {\n            bad = std::max(bad, 1);\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {\n            bad = std::max(bad, 2);\n        }\n        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {\n            bad = std::max(bad, 3);\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (bad == 1) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n    } else if (bad == 2) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n    } else if (bad == 3) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds every rank keeps a full\n//               copy of all positions and owns the velocities/integration of its block.\n//               Positions are exchanged once per step with a single MPI_Allgatherv.\n//   * OpenMP  : one host thread per GPU owned by the rank (drives independent CUDA\n//               streams/devices concurrently) plus host-side parallel loops for\n//               packing, serialization and validation.\n//   * CUDA    : the O(N^2) force evaluation, the integration and the potential-energy\n//               evaluation run on the GPUs, using shared-memory tiling. -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Accumulates the force on the bodies [off, off+cnt) from the j-range of this split.\n// Partial results are stored as acc[split * cnt + i]; grid.y selects the split.\n__global__ void forceKernel(const double3* __restrict__ pos, const int n, const int off, const int cnt,\n                            const int splitLen, double3* __restrict__ acc) {\n    __shared__ double3 tile[BLOCK];\n\n    const int il = blockIdx.x * BLOCK + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = il < cnt;\n\n    const int jbeg = blockIdx.y * splitLen;\n    const int jend = min(n, jbeg + splitLen);\n\n    double3 p = make_double3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);\n    if (active) { p = pos[off + il]; }\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n\n    for (int t = jbeg; t < jend; t += BLOCK) {\n        const int j = t + threadIdx.x;\n        if (j < jend) { tile[threadIdx.x] = pos[j]; }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const int lim = min(BLOCK, jend - t);\n        if (active) {\n#pragma unroll 4\n            for (int k = 0; k < lim; ++k) {\n                const double dx = tile[k].x - p.x;\n                const double dy = tile[k].y - p.y;\n                const double dz = tile[k].z - p.z;\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                // rsqrt() is the double precision reciprocal square root (<= 1 ulp,\n                // i.e. the same accuracy as 1.0/sqrt(x) but far cheaper).\n                const double invDist = rsqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (active) { acc[(size_t)blockIdx.y * cnt + il] = make_double3(Fx, Fy, Fz); }\n}\n\n// Sums the per-split partial forces (in ascending split order) and applies them to the\n// local velocities, then integrates the local positions.\n__global__ void updateKernel(const double3* __restrict__ acc, double3* __restrict__ vel,\n                             double3* __restrict__ pos, const int off, const int cnt, const int nSplit) {\n    const int il = blockIdx.x * BLOCK + threadIdx.x;\n    if (il >= cnt) { return; }\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n    for (int s = 0; s < nSplit; ++s) {\n        const double3 a = acc[(size_t)s * cnt + il];\n        Fx += a.x;\n        Fy += a.y;\n        Fz += a.z;\n    }\n\n    double3 v = vel[il];\n    v.x += DT * Fx;\n    v.y += DT * Fy;\n    v.z += DT * Fz;\n    vel[il] = v;\n\n    double3 p = pos[off + il];\n    p.x += v.x * DT;\n    p.y += v.y * DT;\n    p.z += v.z * DT;\n    pos[off + il] = p;\n}\n\n// Potential energy contribution of every local body i: sum over j > i of -1/dist.\n__global__ void energyKernel(const double3* __restrict__ pos, const int n, const int off, const int cnt,\n                             double* __restrict__ part) {\n    __shared__ double3 tile[BLOCK];\n\n    const int il = blockIdx.x * BLOCK + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = il < cnt;\n    const int i = off + il;\n\n    double3 p = make_double3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);\n    if (active) { p = pos[i]; }\n\n    double e = 0.0;\n    // Only tiles that can contain a j > i of any body in this block need to be visited.\n    // The bound is block-uniform so that __syncthreads() stays convergent.\n    const int tbeg = ((off + blockIdx.x * BLOCK) / BLOCK) * BLOCK;\n    for (int t = tbeg; t < n; t += BLOCK) {\n        const int j = t + threadIdx.x;\n        if (j < n) { tile[threadIdx.x] = pos[j]; }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const int lim = min(BLOCK, n - t);\n        for (int k = 0; k < lim; ++k) {\n            if (t + k <= i) { continue; }\n            const double dx = tile[k].x - p.x;\n            const double dy = tile[k].y - p.y;\n            const double dz = tile[k].z - p.z;\n            const double dist = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            e -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (active) { part[il] = e; }\n}\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Per-GPU state\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nstruct GpuCtx {\n    int dev = 0;\n    int off = 0;  // first body owned by this GPU (global index)\n    int cnt = 0;  // number of bodies owned by this GPU\n    int nSplit = 1;\n    int splitLen = 0;\n    double3* d_pos = nullptr;   // all N positions\n    double3* d_vel = nullptr;   // cnt velocities\n    double3* d_acc = nullptr;   // nSplit * cnt partial forces\n    double* d_epart = nullptr;  // cnt potential energy partials\n    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;\n};\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Host-side helpers (unchanged semantics, OpenMP parallel)\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<double>& potentialParts) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + body.vel.y * body.vel.y + body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies), accumulated in ascending\n    // body order from the per-body partial sums computed on the GPUs.\n    for (const double p : potentialParts) {\n        energy += p;\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    int bad = 0;  // 1: non-finite, 2: position out of bounds, 3: velocity out of bounds\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(max : bad)\n    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < n; ++idx) {\n        const auto& body = bodies[idx];\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||\n            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {\n            bad = std::max(bad, 1);\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {\n            bad = std::max(bad, 2);\n        }\n        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {\n            bad = std::max(bad, 3);\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (bad == 1) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n    } else if (bad == 2) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n    } else if (bad == 3) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n    }\n    return bad == 0;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int mpiProvided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &mpiProvided);\n    (void)mpiProvided;  // MPI calls are funnelled through the master thread\n\n    int rank = 0, nRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nRanks);\n\n    int numBodies = 1024;\n    int numSteps = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) { printUsage(argv[0]); }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"N-Body Simulation\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of bodies: %d\\n\", numBodies);\n        printf(\"Number of steps: %d\\n\", numSteps);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    if (numBodies <= 0) {\n        if (rank == 0) { MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &nodeRank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(nodeComm, &nodeSize);\n\n    int devCount = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&devCount));\n    if (devCount == 0) {\n        fprintf(stderr,                     cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, c.stream));\n                }\n                CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(c.stream));\n\n#pragma omp barrier\n#pragma omp master\n                {\n recvDispls.data(), MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n                    }\n                }\n#pragma omp barrier\n\n                // Fetch the positions owned by the other GPUs/ranks; the own block Each thread\n//               accumulates the interactions of one body in ascending j order so the\n//               floating point summation order matches the original serial code.\n\n#include results are stored as acc[split * cnt + i]; grid.y selects the split.\n__global__ void forceKernel(const double3* __restrict__ pos, const int n, const int off, const int cnt,\n                            const int splitLen, double3* __restrict__ acc) {\n    __shared__ double3 tile[BLOCK];\n\n    // Every thread handles ILP bodies to expose instruction level parallelism (the\n    // FP64 pipeline is deep and narrow) and to amortize the shared memory traffic.\n    const int il0 = blockIdx.x * (BLOCK * ILP) + threadIdx.x;\n\n    const int jbeg = blockIdx.y * splitLen;\n    const int jend = min(n, jbeg + splitLen);\n\n    double px[ILP], py[ILP], pz[ILP];\n    double Fx[ILP], Fy[ILP], Fz[ILP];\n#pragma unroll\n    for (int m = 0; m < ILP; ++m) {\n        // Out of range lanes read a valid body                double3* __restrict__ pos, const int off, const int cnt, const int nSplit) {\n    const int il = blockIdx.x * BLOCK + threadIdx.x;\n    if (il >= cnt) { return; }\n\n    double Fx = = e; }\n}\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Per-GPU state\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Host-side helpers (unchanged semantics, OpenMP parallel)\n// energy = 0.0;\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + body.vel.y * body.vel.y + body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies), accumulated in ascending\n    // body order from the per-body partial sums computed on the GPUs.\n    for (const double p : potentialParts) {\n        energy += p;\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    int bad = 0;  // 1: non-finite, 2: position out of bounds, 3: velocity out of bounds\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(max :     }\n\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {\n            bad = std::max(bad, 2);\n        }\n        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {\n            bad = std::max(bad, 3);\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (bad == 1) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n    } reasonable bounds\\n\");\n    }\n    return bad == 0;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n     printf(\"Number of steps: %d\\n\", numSteps);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    if (numBodies <= 0) {\n        if (rank == 0) { 1) / nodeSize);\n    if (devEnd > devBeg) {\n        for (int d = devBeg; d < devEnd; ++d) {\n            myDevs.push_back(d);\n        }\n    } else {\n        myDevs.push_back(nodeRank % devCount);\n    }\n    // Never use more GPUs than there are bodies to compute.\n    if ((int)myDevs.size() > myCnt && myCnt > 0) { myDevs.resize(myCnt); }\n    const int nGpu = (int)myDevs.size();\n    const int devFirst = myDevs[0];\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"MPI ranks: %d, GPUs per rank: %d, OpenMP threads: %d\\n\", nRanks, nGpu, omp_get_max_threads());\n    }\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    std::vector<Body> bodies(n);\n    randomizeBodies(bodies);\n\n    double3* h_pos = nullptr;  // all N positions, pinned for std::vector<GpuCtx> ctx(nGpu);\n    for (int g = 0; g < nGpu; ++g) {\n        ctx[g].dev = myDevs[g];\n        ctx[g].off = myOff + (int)((long long)myCnt * g / nGpu);\n        ctx[g].cnt = myOff + CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&c.stream));\n\n        // Choose the number of j-splits so that enough threads are resident even for\n        // small body counts.\n        int nSplit = 1;\n        if (c.cnt > 0) {\n            nSplit = std::max(1, MIN_THREADS / c.cnt);\n            nSplit = std::min(nSplit, MAX_SPLIT);\n            nSplit = std::min(nSplit, std::max(1, n / MIN_SPLIT_LEN));\n CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc((void**)&c.d_vel, (size_t)c.cnt * sizeof(double3)));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc((void**)&c.d_acc, (size_t)nSplit * c.cnt * sizeof(double3)));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(c.d_vel, h_vel + (c.off - myOff), (size_t)c.cnt * sizeof(double3),\n                                       cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, c.stream));\n        }\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(c.d_pos, h_pos, (size_t)n * sizeof(double3), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, c.stream));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(c.stream));\n    }\n\n    // MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n#pragma omp parallel num_threads(nGpu)\n    {\n        const int g = omp_get_thread_num();\n        GpuCtx& BLOCK);\n\n        for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {\n            if (c.cnt > 0) {\n                forceKernel<<<forceGrid, BLOCK, 0, c.stream>>>(c.d_pos, n, c.off, c.cnt, c.splitLen, Publish the positions of the locally owned bodies.\n                if (c.cnt > 0) {\n                    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(h_pos + c.off, c.d_pos + c.off, (size_t)c.cnt * sizeof(double3),\n Each thread\n//               accumulates the interactions of one body in ascending j order so the\n//               floating point summation order matches the original serial code.\n\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nconstexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;\nconstexpr double DT = 0.01;\n\n// CUDA tuning parameters\nconstexpr int BLOCK = 128;         // threads per block (also the tile size)\nconstexpr int MIN_THREADS = 65536; // target amount of force threads before splitting j\nconstexpr int MIN_SPLIT_LEN = 512; // minimum j-range handled by one j-split\nconstexpr int MAX_SPLIT = 64;      // maximum number of j-splits\n\nstruct Vec3 {\n    double x, y, z;\n    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}\n};\n\nstruct Body {\n    Vec3 pos;\n    Vec3 vel;\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                                     \\\n    do {                                                                                                     \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                                      \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                            \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_), __FILE__, __LINE__);        \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                                     \\\n        }                                                                                                    \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Device kernels\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// Accumulates the force on the bodies [off, off+cnt) from the j-range of this split.\n// Partial results are stored as acc[split * cnt + i]; grid.y selects the split.\n__global__ void forceKernel(const double3* __restrict__ pos, const int n, const int off, const int cnt,\n                            const int splitLen, double3* __restrict__ acc) {\n    __shared__ double3 tile[BLOCK];\n\n    const int il = blockIdx.x * BLOCK + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = il < cnt;\n\n    const int jbeg = blockIdx.y * splitLen;\n    const int jend = min(n, jbeg + splitLen);\n\n    double3 p = make_double3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);\n    if (active) { p = pos[off + il]; }\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n\n    for (int t = jbeg; t < jend; t += BLOCK) {\n        const int j = t + threadIdx.x;\n        if (j < jend) { tile[threadIdx.x] = pos[j]; }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const int lim = min(BLOCK, jend - t);\n        if (active) {\n#pragma unroll 4\n            for (int k = 0; k < lim; ++k) {\n                const double dx = tile[k].x - p.x;\n                const double dy = tile[k].y - p.y;\n                const double dz = tile[k].z - p.z;\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                // rsqrt() is the double precision reciprocal square root (<= 1 ulp,\n                // i.e. the same accuracy as 1.0/sqrt(x) but far cheaper).\n                const double invDist = rsqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (active) { acc[(size_t)blockIdx.y * cnt + il] = make_double3(Fx, Fy, Fz); }\n}\n\n// Sums the per-split partial forces (in ascending split order) and applies them to the\n// local velocities, then integrates the local positions.\n__global__ void updateKernel(const double3* __restrict__ acc, double3* __restrict__ vel,\n                             double3* __restrict__ pos, const int off, const int cnt, const int nSplit) {\n    const int il = blockIdx.x * BLOCK + threadIdx.x;\n    if (il >= cnt) { return; }\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n    for (int s = 0; s < nSplit; ++s) {\n        const double3 a = acc[(size_t)s * cnt + il];\n        Fx += a.x;\n        Fy += a.y;\n        Fz += a.z;\n    }\n\n    double3 v = vel[il];\n    v.x += DT * Fx;\n    v.y += DT * Fy;\n    v.z += DT * Fz;\n    vel[il] = v;\n\n    double3 p = pos[off + il];\n    p.x += v.x * DT;\n    p.y += v.y * DT;\n    p.z += v.z * DT;\n    pos[off + il] = p;\n}\n\n// Potential energy contribution of every local body i: sum over j > i of -1/dist.\n__global__ void energyKernel(const double3* __restrict__ pos, const int n, const int off, const int cnt,\n                             double* __restrict__ part) {\n    __shared__ double3 tile[BLOCK];\n\n    const int il = blockIdx.x * BLOCK + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = il < cnt;\n    const int i = off + il;\n\n    double3 p = make_double3(0.0, 0.0, 0.0);\n    if (active) { p = pos[i]; }\n\n    double e = 0.0;\n    // Only tiles that can contain a j > i of any body in this block need to be visited.\n    // The bound is block-uniform so that __syncthreads() stays convergent.\n    const int tbeg = ((off + blockIdx.x * BLOCK) / BLOCK) * BLOCK;\n    for (int t = tbeg; t < n; t += BLOCK) {\n        const int j = t + threadIdx.x;\n        if (j < n) { tile[threadIdx.x] = pos[j]; }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const int lim = min(BLOCK, n - t);\n        for (int k = 0; k < lim; ++k) {\n            if (t + k <= i) { continue; }\n            const double dx = tile[k].x - p.x;\n            const double dy = tile[k].y - p.y;\n            const double dz = tile[k].z - p.z;\n            const double dist = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            e -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (active) { part[il] = e; }\n}\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Per-GPU state\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nstruct GpuCtx {\n    int dev = 0;\n    int off = 0;  // first body owned by this GPU (global index)\n    int cnt = 0;  // number of bodies owned by this GPU\n    int nSplit = 1;\n    int splitLen = 0;\n    double3* d_pos = nullptr;   // all N positions\n    double3* d_vel = nullptr;   // cnt velocities\n    double3* d_acc = nullptr;   // nSplit * cnt partial forces\n    double* d_epart = nullptr;  // cnt potential energy partials\n    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;\n};\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Host-side helpers (unchanged semantics, OpenMP parallel)\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<double>& potentialParts) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + body.vel.y * body.vel.y + body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies), accumulated in ascending\n    // body order from the per-body partial sums computed on the GPUs.\n    for (const double p : potentialParts) {\n        energy += p;\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    int bad = 0;  // 1: non-finite, 2: position out of bounds, 3: velocity out of bounds\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(max : bad)\n    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < n; ++idx) {\n        const auto& body = bodies[idx];\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||\n            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {\n            bad = std::max(bad, 1);\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {\n            bad = std::max(bad, 2);\n        }\n        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {\n            bad = std::max(bad, 3);\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (bad == 1) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n    } else if (bad == 2) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n    } else if (bad == 3) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n    }\n    return bad == 0;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int mpiProvided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &mpiProvided);\n    (void)mpiProvided;  // MPI calls are funnelled through the master thread\n\n    int rank = 0, nRanks = precision reciprocal square root (<= 1 ulp,\n                // i.e. the same accuracy as 1.0/sqrt(x) but far cheaper).\n                const double invDist = rsqrt(distSqr);\n                const __restrict__ pos, const int off, const int cnt, const int nSplit) {\n    const int il = blockIdx.x * BLOCK + threadIdx.x;\n    if (il >= cnt) { return; }\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n   // The bound is block-uniform so that __syncthreads() stays convergent.\n    const int tbeg = ((off + blockIdx.x * BLOCK) / BLOCK) * BLOCK;\n    for (int t = tbeg; t < n; t += BLOCK) {\n        const int j = t + threadIdx.x;\n        if (j < n) { tile[threadIdx.x] = pos[j]; }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const int lim = min(BLOCK, n - t);\n        for (int k = 0; k < lim; ++k) {\n energy partials\n    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;\n};\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Host-side helpers (unchanged semantics, OpenMP parallel)\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<double>& potentialParts) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + body.vel.y * body.vel.y + body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies), accumulated in ascending\n    // body order from the per-body partial sums computed on the GPUs.\n    for (const double p : potentialParts) {\n        energy += p;\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    int bad = 0;  // 1: non-finite, 2: position out of bounds, 3: velocity out of bounds\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(max : bad)\n    for (size_t idx = 0; idx < n; ++idx) {\n        const auto& body = bodies[idx];\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||\n            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {\n            bad = std::max(bad, 1);\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {\n            bad = std::max(bad, 2);\n        }\n        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {\n            bad = std::max(bad, 3);\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (bad == 1) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n    } else if (bad == 2) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n    } else if (bad == 3) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n    }\n    return bad == 0;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int mpiProvided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &mpiProvided);\n    (void)mpiProvided;  // MPI calls are funnelled through the master thread\n\n    int rank = 0, nRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nRanks);\n\n    int numBodies = 1024;\n    int numSteps = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) { printUsage(argv[0]); }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n     printf(\"Number of steps: %d\\n\", numSteps);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    if (numBodies <= 0) {\n        if (rank == 0) { printf(\"Simulation time: 0 ms\\n\"); }\n        MPI_Finalize();\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    const int n = numBodies;\n\n    // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n    std::vector<int> rankOff(nRanks + 1);\n    for (int r = 0; r <= nRanks; ++r) {\n        rankOff[r] = (int)((long long)n * r / nRanks);\n    }\n    const int myOff = rankOff[rank];\n    const int myCnt = rankOff[rank + 1] - myOff;\n\n    // Determine how many GPUs this rank may use: devices are shared out among the\n    // ranks that run on the same node.\n    MPI_Comm nodeComm;\n    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm);\n    int nodeRank = 0, nodeSize = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &nodeRank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(nodeComm, &nodeSize);\n\n    int devCount = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&devCount));\n    if (devCount == 0) {\n        fprintf(stderr, 1) / nodeSize);\n    if (devEnd > devBeg) {\n        for (int d = devBeg; d < devEnd; ++d) {\n            myDevs.push_back(d);\n        }\n    } else {\n        myDevs.push_back(nodeRank % hostThreads ? cudaDeviceScheduleYield : cudaDeviceScheduleSpin));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreate(&c.stream));\n\n        // Choose the number of j-splits so that enough threads are resident even {}\n};\n\nstruct Body {\n    Vec3 pos;\n    Vec3 vel;\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x = 2.0 * precision reciprocal square root (<= 1 ulp,\n                // i.e. the same accuracy as 1.0/sqrt(x) but far cheaper).\n                const double invDist = rsqrt(distSqr);\n                const                double* __restrict__ part) {\n    __shared__ double3 tile[BLOCK];\n\n    const int il = blockIdx.x * BLOCK + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = il < cnt;\n    const int i = off +   // The bound is block-uniform so that __syncthreads() stays convergent.\n    const int tbeg = ((off + blockIdx.x * BLOCK) / BLOCK) * BLOCK;\n    for (int t = tbeg; t < n; t += BLOCK) {\n parallel)\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<double>& potentialParts) {\n body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies), accumulated in ascending\n    // body order from the per-body partial sums computed on the GPUs.\n    for (const {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011Cefsx5JN1Q9y3bijsEwkj","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done. The benchmark now runs unconditionally as an MPI + OpenMP + CUDA hybrid, with only `nbody/nbody.cpp` and `nbody/CMakeLists.txt` changed (no new files, executable still `nbody`).\n\n## Parallelization design\n\n**MPI** — the body array is block-distributed over ranks. Each rank owns the velocities and integration of its block and keeps a full copy of all positions; one `MPI_Allgatherv` (in-place, pinned host buffer) per step exchanges positions. Rank/GPU assignment uses `MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED)` so devices are shared out among the ranks on a node (and shared round-robin if ranks > GPUs).\n\n**OpenMP** — one host thread per GPU the rank owns, each driving its own device and stream, so a single rank saturates all node GPUs regardless of `-np`. Host-side loops (packing, serialization, validation) are OpenMP-parallel.\n\n**CUDA** — the O(N²) force loop, the integration and the potential-energy evaluation run on the GPUs with shared-memory tiling. One thread per body accumulates over `j` in ascending order, so the summation order matches the original serial loop; an adaptive j-split (partial forces reduced in ascending split order) keeps the GPUs busy for small N.\n\nKey tuning results (RTX 3090 ×4): `BLOCK=128`, `#pragma unroll 4`, no register blocking (ILP 2/4 were 12–28 % *slower*), double-precision `rsqrt` instead of `1.0/sqrt` (≤1 ulp, same accuracy class, 27 % faster), j-splitting (3.7× on N=1024), kernel pre-loading out of the timed region, and spin/yield sync policy chosen from the core:GPU ratio (fixes a 70 % regression when `mpirun` binds a rank to few cores).\n\n## Correctness\n- Results are **identical across all tested configurations** (1/2/3/4/5/8 ranks, 1–4 GPUs, standalone and under `mpirun`) and match the serial reference to ~1e-12 relative on positions/velocities and ~1e-11 on the energy — pure FP reassociation/`rsqrt` noise, no bit-exactness claim.\n- Edge cases verified against the reference: N = 0, 1, 2, 3, 5, 7, 100, 1024, 1025, 4097, non-multiples of the tile size, `-s 0`, more ranks than bodies, `-h`, unknown options, and exit codes.\n- I found and fixed one race during development: a thread's H2D read of the shared host position buffer could overlap the next step's D2H write by another thread (visible as nondeterministic output when GPUs outnumber cores); the exchange now syncs and barriers after the fetch.\n\n## Performance (4× RTX 3090, vs. serial `-O3 -march=native`)\n\n| N (10 steps) | serial | 1 GPU | 4 GPUs | speedup vs serial |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| 1 024 | 77 ms | 1 ms | 2 ms | ~40× |\n| 4 096 | 1 238 ms | 12 ms | 5 ms | 248× |\n| 16 384 | 19 887 ms | 178 ms | 51 ms | 390× |\n| 65 536 | ~320 s (extrap.) | 2 584 ms | 686 ms | ~460× |\n\nMulti-GPU scaling is 3.77–3.88× on 4 GPUs for N ≥ 16k; at N ≈ 1k the per-step exchange dominates, so a single GPU is faster there.
